Detailed gradient for softmax plus LSE:
Let u vector (length n). a = exp(u - logsumexp(u)). Sum1 positive. c = conv(a,a) length L=2n-1. m = max(c). We can ignore constant normalization.
Gradient of F wrt a: For c_k, derivative dc_k/da_i = 2 a_{k-i} (if 0≤k-i<n). g_a_i = 2 * ∑_k P_k a_{k-i} (where P=p/beta? Can we compute gradient alternatively in time domain for active top peaks only? If P effectively on top K peaks (high beta, p concentrated), gradient =4n ∑_{top K} w_k a_{k-i}, can compute sparse O(Kn) for K small. For high beta, active peaks maybe few, use top K weights.    a = norm(a0); u? We'll work with a directly.

      S=a.sum(); if S==0 break; a=a/S maybe after update.
      cmax=c.max(); sc=2n*cmax/(S*S)

      d = g - dot
      # clip d to avoid huge relative changes: scale = max(step_size,?)

Spectral square-root cap algorithm:
- Input a, cap_factor, alpha, iterations.
- For each iter:
   compute c, L, M=max(c), sumc.
   If M <= target? break.
   cap = M * cap_factor (or maybe best_score/(2n) * margin). But if cap too low, target sum? We'll target t.
   high = c > cap
   deficit = (c[high]-cap).sum()
   t = c.copy(); t[high]=cap; t += deficit / L  # redistribute uniformly
   # But adding uniformly raises low peaks; could instead add to min values or active smoothed.
   # Compute FFT target c: B_tgt = rfft(t, M) (length L zero pad). B_curr = A*A.
   ratio mag = |B_tgt|/|B_curr|, phase diff.
   log ratio = log(max(|B_tgt|/|B_curr|, tiny)) + 1j*(angle(B_tgt)-angle(B_curr))
   A_new = A * np.exp(0.5*alpha*log_ratio)
   a_new = ifft(A_new)[:n]; a_new=max(real,0); normalize.
   If worse, reduce alpha? Track best.

Let's design a `gradient_from_weights(a, weights_lags, n, weights)` function:

Implementation optimize:
```
g = np.zeros(n)
for idx,w in zip(lags, weights):
   if w==0: continue
   k=int(idx)
   js = max(0, k-n+1)
   je = min(k, n-1)
   g[js:je+1] += (4.0*n*w) * a[js:je+1][::-1]
```

Spectral square-root cap: Might be expensive but can give quick max reduction and set beta. Let's implement a simpler version to use before MLSE:
```
def spectral_cap(a0, time_limit, cap_ratio=0.98, alpha=0.1):
   a=norm(a0); n; M1=...
   while time<end:
      A=fft; c=max(0,irfft(A*A)[:L])
      M=c.max(); sc=...
      update best
      cap = M * cap_ratio
      # If cap < mean*? choose
      t = c.copy()
      high = c > cap
      if not high.any(): break
      deficit = (t[high]-cap).sum()
      t[high] = cap
      t += deficit / L
      # ensure nonneg
      Bt = fft(t,M1)[:?]
      ratio_amplitude = np.abs(Bt) / (np.abs(A*A)+1e-30)
      phase_diff = np.angle(Bt) - np.angle(A*A)
      # update A = A * exp(0.5*alpha*(log_ratio + i phase_diff))
      log_ratio = np.log(np.maximum(ratio_amplitude,1e-12))
      A = A * np.exp(0.5*alpha*(log_ratio + 1j*phase_diff))
      a_new = np.fft.irfft(A, M1)[:n].real
      a_new = np.maximum(a_new, floor); normalize
      if score maybe accepted? Track best; if best not improve patience reduce alpha
   return best
```
But Bt = FFT(t) zero-padded to M1. A*A is exactly B_curr in freq. Since t modified time, Bt different. Using A from a; A*A. The ratio is complex. We need not unwrap? angle diff may have branch issues; if ratio near zero, phase noisy. Instead use geometric update: A_new = A * (Bt / (A*A + eps)) ** (0.5 alpha) using complex power? `np.power` with complex exponent maybe branch. Better use log. If Bt magnitude zero? t nonnegative sum1, Bt not all zero; some freq zeros possible. log ratio for zeros: set to 0 (no change). Use mask = abs(Bt)>1e-12 & abs(Bcurr)>1e-12.
```
ratio = Bt / (Bcurr + 1e-30)
# where ratio small/large log; phase diff
mask = np.isfinite(ratio) & (np.abs(ratio)>1e-12)
logr = np.zeros_like(ratio, complex?)
logr[mask] = np.log(np.abs(ratio[mask])) + 1j*np.angle(ratio[mask])
A *= np.exp(0.5*alpha*logr)
```
This updates phase. Could be good. But if t differs only capping, ratio close1; alpha small.

Detailed helper functions:

```
def _pow2(x):
    if x <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_pos(a, floor=0.0)`:
```
a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
if a.size==0: return np.ones(64)/64
if floor>0: a = np.maximum(a, floor)
s = a.sum()
if s <=1e-15: return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
return a/s
```
But for optimizer, if floor too high changes; use separate.

`fast_linear_conv(a, m1=None)`:
```
n=a.size; L=2*n-1
if m1 is None: m1=_pow2(L)
A=np.fft.rfft(a, m1)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, m1)[:L]
# remove tiny negative
np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
return c
```
If a not normalized, c scale.

`exact_score(a)`:
```
n=a.size; s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12: return inf
c=fast_conv(a); return 2.0*n*float(c.max())/(s*s)
```
Could use c and S for speed in loops.

`lse_smooth(c, beta)`:
```
cm = float(c.max())
shifted = beta*(c-cm)
e = np.exp(shifted)
se = float(e.sum())
if se <=0: p=np.zeros_like(c); im=c.argmax(); p[im]=1; L=cm
else: p=e/se; L=cm + np.log(se)/beta
return L,p,cm
```
If beta=0? not used.

`grad_from_p(a,p,m2)`:
```
n=a.size; 
if m2 is None: m2=_pow2(3*n-2)
PA=np.fft.rfft(p,m2)
RA=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],m2)
g=np.fft.irfft(PA*RA,m2)[n-1:2*n-1]
return g
```
Check segment length: 2n-1 - (n-1)+1 = n. Good.

`sparse_grad_from_lags(a,lags,w,mult=4.0)`:
```
n=a.size; g=np.zeros(n)
for idx,weight in zip(lags,w):
  if weight ==0: continue
  k=int(idx)
  js=0 if k>=n-1 else k-n+1
  je=k if k<n-1 else n-1
  if je>=js:
    g[js:je+1] += mult*weight * a[js:je+1][::-1]
return g
```
Use mult=4.0*n? We'll include 4.0*n in function: `g[js:je] += (4.0*n*weight)*...`

`select_weights(c, K, beta, cmax=None)`:
```
L=c.size
if K>=L: idx=np.arange(L)
else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
# subset maybe order; cmax over subset for weights? Use global max if given.
if cmax is None: cmax=c.max()
vals=c[idx]
w=np.exp(beta*(vals-cmax))
sw=w.sum()
if sw<=0:
  w=np.ones_like(vals); sw=L? 
else: w=w/sw
return idx,w
```
If beta large, for values equal max w=1; good.

`mlse_update` phase: Could implement a generic `run_mlse(a0, seconds, mode='full', beta_factor=20, strength=0.15, patience=150, K=256, print_tag='')`.
Pseudo:
```
def run_mlse(a0, seconds, beta_factor=20.0, strength0=0.15, patience=200, max_strength=0.35, min_strength=0.01, mode='auto', K_full=1024, K_sparse=128, print_tag='MLSE', verbose_interval=200):
    a = normalize_pos(a0, floor=0.0)
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    m1=_pow2(L); m2=_pow2(3*n-2)
    use_full = (m2 <= 65536 or n <= 20000) and mode!='sparse'
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=2n*max(conv)/S^2...
    best_sc? compute c initial? We'll compute in loop; initialize by exact_score(a)
    strength=strength0
    noimp=0; it=0; last_print=0
    t_end=time.time()+seconds
    floor=1e-12
    while time.time()<t_end:
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,m1); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,m1)[:L]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
       # c sum maybe 1 if a normalized; due float small. If S not1, we normalize a before? We maintain.
       S=float(a.sum())
       if S<=0: break
       a = a/S
       # If after normalization c scaled by S^2? But A computed from a before S? We normalized after; if S close1 (1) no issue. Better ensure a normalized before FFT: at start of loop a= a/a.sum(); then compute A/c. Let's do that.
```
At start: `a = a / a.sum()` (sum1). Then c sum1. Sc = 2n cmax.
```
       cmax=c.max(); sc=2*n*cmax
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); noimp=0
          # optionally strength = min(max_strength, strength*1.02)
       else: noimp+=1
       if it % interval ==0: print tag, sc, strength
       # beta = beta_factor*n*(0.5 + min(1,it/5000)) maybe start lower? If beta too high from start, top weights narrow. Use beta = beta_factor*n
       beta = beta_factor * n
       Lval, p, cmax = lse_smooth(c,beta)
       if use_full:
           g=grad_from_p(a,p,m2)
       else:
           K = min(K_sparse, max(16, int(0.01*L)))
           idx,w = select_weights(c,K,beta)
           # Since p already full, we could restrict p; but select weights may duplicate. Use p[idx] normalized? Better use p[top] to incorporate beta.
           top = idx # from p? p array already normalized over all lags; for sparse, use top p weights:
           if K < L: idx = np.argpartition(p, -K)[-K:]  # weight distribution over lags, not c? P is exp weighted; top p same as top c for beta>0.
           w = p[idx]/np.sum(p[idx])
           g=sparse_grad_from_lags(a, idx, w)
       # Ensure g finite
       g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
       dot = float(np.dot(a, g))
       d = g - dot
       scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
       if scale < 1e-15:
          # no movement; reduce strength maybe reset
          if noimp > patience//2: break? or reduce
          break? We'll break phase.
       exponent = -strength * d / scale
       # If strength > max_exp, clip: already max exponent=strength. If strength >0.5, clip to +/-0.5.
       max_exp=0.5
       if strength > max_exp: exponent = np.clip(exponent, -max_exp, max_exp)
       # Alternatively if strength small, no clip
       a_new = a * np.exp(exponent)
       a_new = np.maximum(a_new, floor)
       s_new = a_new.sum()
       if s_new <= 0: break
       a_new = a_new / s_new
       a = a_new
       it+=1
       if noimp > patience:
           strength *= 0.6
           noimp = 0
           if strength < min_strength:
               # reset to best and try again with min*2
               a = best_a.copy()
               strength = min_strength*2
               noimp=0
               if time.time() > t_end - 20: break
           elif float(np.random.rand()) < 0.1: # small perturb?
               pass
    # final if best not updated? ensure a maybe best? return best
    return best_a, best_sc, best_a? 
```

Spectral cap implementation:
```
def run_spectral_cap(a0, seconds, cap_ratio=0.98, alpha=0.05, speed=1.0, print_tag='SPEC'):
  a=normalize; n; M1=_pow2(2n-1); L=2n-1; best...
  strength=alpha; noimp=0; it=0; t_end=...
  while time<t_end:
    A=rfft(a,M1); c=irfft(A*A)[:L]; c=max(c,0)
    sc=2n*c.max()
    update best
    M=c.max(); cap=M*cap_ratio
    # To avoid cap<=0, cap = max(cap, M*0.5)
    t=c.copy()
    high = c>cap
    if not high.any(): break
    deficit=(c[high]-cap).sum()
    t[high]=cap
    t += deficit/(L)
    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    t_sum=t.sum()
    if t_sum<=0: break
    # t should sum 1; if not, rescale
    t /= t_sum
    Bcurr = A*A
    Bt = np.fft.rfft(t, M1)
    denom = Bcurr + 1e-30
    ratio = Bt / denom
    mask = np.isfinite(ratio) & (np.abs(ratio)>1e-12)
    altr = np.zeros_like(ratio, dtype=np.complex128)
    logr = np.log(np.abs(ratio[mask])) + 1j*np.angle(ratio[mask])
    altr[mask]=logr
    # if cap ratio close1, alter small; update
    A_new = A * np.exp(0.5 * strength * altr)
    a_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new, M1)[:n].real
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-12)
    s=a_new.sum(); if s<=0 break; a_new/=s
    a=a_new; it++
    if noimp>100: strength*=0.7; noimp=0; if strength<1e-3: a=best_a.copy(); strength=1e-2; maybe break
  return best
```

```

def _target_flatten(a0, seconds, mix=0.15, alpha0=0.05, tag='FLAT'):
   a = normalize; n; m1=pow2; best
   alpha=alpha0; mult=1.0; noimp=0
   while time:

      t = (1-mix)*c + mix/(2*n-1)
      Bt = rfft(t,m1)
      Bc = A*A
      ratio = Bt/(Bc+1e-30)
      mask = np.isfinite(ratio) & (abs>1e-12)
      alter = zeros complex
      alter[mask] = log(abs) + 1j angle
      A = A * exp(0.5*alpha*alter)
      a_new = irfft(A,m1)[:n].real
      a_new = maximum(a_new, floor); normalize
      a=a_new; it++
      if noimp>150: alpha*=0.7; mult?; if alpha<1e-3 break/reset
   return best

def _resample(a,n_new):
   a=np.asarray(a).ravel(); n_old=a.size
   if n_old == n_new: return a
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n_old-1) if n_old>1 else [0]
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new-1)
   y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   # smooth
   kernel=np.array([0.2,0.3,0.2,0.3,0.2])? sum1? maybe [0.1,0.2,0.4,0.2,0.1]
   if n_new>4: y=np.convolve(y,kernel,mode='same')
   return _normalize(y, floor=1e-14/n_new)
```

def _one_sided(n, delta=0.5, alpha=0.5, lam=0.0, support_frac=1.0):

    x=(idx+delta)/max(1.0,n-1.0)

Let's test mental code for `_grad_full` segment:
Given p length L, rev length n. Convolution length L+n-1. We want for each i=0..n-1, ∑_{k=0}^{L-1} p_k a_{k-i} where k-i in [0,n-1]. In conv(p, rev) index t: ∑_k p_k rev[t-k]. t = i+n-1: rev[i+n-1-k]=a[n-1-(i+n-1-k)]=a[k-i]. If k-i can be negative? rev index outside [0,n-1] zero due zero padding, so good. Segment `n-1:2*n-1` length n. Good.

Now LSE with c from a sum1. P sum1. `dot = np.dot(a,g)`. Since ∑ a*g can be computed as 4n ∑_i a_i ∑_k p_k a_{k-i} =4n ∑_k p_k ∑_i a_i a_{k-i} =4n ∑ p_k c_k. Since p sum1, dot=4n ∑ p c. This is weighted average of c times 4n. Good. d_i =4n(∑ p a_{k-i} -∑ p c). For coordinates contributing to weighted peaks, d positive.

Detailed code:

```
def _pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`_normalize(a, floor=0.0)`:
```
a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
if a.size == 0:
    a = np.ones(64)
a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
if floor > 0.0:
    a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
s = float(a.sum())
if s <= 1e-15:
    return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
return a/s
```

`_fast_conv` as above. Note: If a size=1, m1=1, rfft length1; L=1; works.

`_score(a)`:
```
def _score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-15: return np.inf
    c=_fast_conv(a)
    return float(2.0*a.size*float(c.max())/(s*s))
```

`_better`:
```
def _better(sc, a, best_sc, best_a, tag=''):
    if sc < best_sc:
        print(f"[{tag}] NEW BEST {sc:.10f} len={len(a)} t={time.monotonic()-_GLOBAL_T0:.0f}s" if tag != '' else ...)
        return True, a.copy(), sc
    return False, best_a, best_sc
```
Need global T0? We can avoid printing time in helper to not need global. Just print tag, sc. Or use no time. But progress time useful. We can use a top-level variable `PROPOSE_START` set in propose. Helper can read global. That's okay (no closure). Define `PROPOSE_START = 0.0` top-level. In propose set. In `_better`, if PROPOSE_START? Fine.

But final code with global mutable? okay.

`_lse`:
```
def _lse(c, beta):
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm) or cm <= 0.0:
       p=np.zeros_like(c); p[0]=1; return cm,p,cm
    e = np.exp(beta*(c-cm))
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 1e-300:
       idx = int(np.argmax(c)); p=np.zeros_like(c); p[idx]=1
    else:
       p=e/se
       cm += np.log(se)/beta
    return cm,p,cm
```
Return Lse as `cm` after adjustment; but second return? Let's define returns `Lval, p, cmax`.
```
def _lse(c,beta):
    cmax = float(c.max())
    e=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax))
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <=0: p=zeros; p[argmax]=1; L=cmax
    else: p=e/se; L=cmax+np.log(se)/beta
    return L,p,cmax
```

`_grad_full`:
```
def _grad_full(a,p,m2):
    n=a.size
    if m2 is None: m2=_pow2(3*n-2)
    PA=np.fft.rfft(p,m2)
    RA=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],m2)
    g=np.fft.irfft(PA*RA,m2)[n-1:2*n-1]
    return g
```

`_top_weights`:
```
def _top_weights(c,K,beta):
    L=c.size
    if K <=0: K=1
    if K >= L:
       idx=np.arange(L)
       w=np.ones(L); return idx,w/L
    idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    vals=c[idx]
    vmax=float(vals.max())
    w=np.exp(beta*(vals-vmax))
    sw=float(w.sum())
    if sw <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sw):
       w=np.ones(L)
       idx=np.arange(L)
       w = w / L
    else:
       w=w/sw
    return idx,w
```
If K large e.g. 2000, argpartition okay. For n large sparse K~64.

`_grad_sparse`:
```
def _grad_sparse(a, idx, w):
    n=a.size
    g=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    mult=4.0*float(n)
    for ii in range(idx.size):
       weight=float(w[ii])
       if weight ==0.0: continue
       k=int(idx[ii])
       js = k - n + 1
       if js < 0: js = 0
       je = k
       if je > n-1: je = n-1
       if je >= js:
          coef = mult*weight
          g[js:je+1] += coef * a[js:je+1][::-1]
    return g
```
Potential performance: Slicing `a[js:je+1][::-1]` creates view negative stride; multiplication creates array; 64 times okay. Could pre-reverse local? Fine.

`_mlse`: implement with parameters as above. Use `use_full` default auto.
```
def _mlse(a0, seconds, tag='', strength0=0.15, patience=150, min_strength=0.01, use_full=True, beta_start=8.0, beta_end=40.0, sparse_k=64):
    seconds = max(0.0, float(seconds))
    if seconds <= 0:
       a=_normalize(a0); return a, _score(a)
    a=_normalize(a0, floor=1e-14/max(1,a.size))
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    m1=_pow2(L); m2=_pow2(3*n-2)
    full = bool(use_full)
    if full and (m2 > 65536 or m1 > 65536): full=False
    t_start=time.monotonic(); t_end=t_start+seconds
    # initial c and best
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,m1); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,m1)[:L]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
    sc=float(2.0*n*c.max())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
    if tag: print(f"[{tag}] start {sc:.10f} n={n} full={full} tlim={seconds:.0f}s")
    mult=1.0; noimp=0; it=0
    last_print=0
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       A=... c=...
       sc=...
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy(); noimp=0
          if tag and sc < best_sc -? no
       else:
          noimp +=1
       frac = (time.monotonic()-t_start)/max(1e-9, seconds)
       if frac >1: frac=1
       base_strength = strength0 * (1.0 - 0.4*frac)
       strength = max(min_strength, base_strength*mult)
       if full:
          beta = n*(beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*frac)
          Lser,p,cmax = _lse(c,beta)
          g=_grad_full(a,p,m2)
       else:
          K = max(16, min(128, int(sparse_k)))? maybe sparse_k param
          # choose K based on L: K = int(min(128, max(16, 0.002*L)))
          K = int(min(128, max(16, 0.002*L)))
          beta = n*(beta_start + (beta_end-beta_start)*frac)
          idx,w=_top_weights(c,K,beta)
          g=_grad_sparse(a,idx,w)
       # clean
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
           g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
       dot = float(np.dot(a,g))
       d = g - dot
       scale = float(np.std(d))
       if scale <= 1e-15:
           # tiny movement; reduce
           noimp += 5
           if noimp > patience:
              mult *=0.5
              noimp=0
              if mult < 0.1: break
           continue
       exponent = -strength * d / scale
       max_exp=0.6
       if strength > max_exp:
           exponent = np.clip(exponent, -max_exp, max_exp)
       else:
           # still clip for safety
           exponent = np.clip(exponent, -1.5, 1.5)
       a_new = a * np.exp(exponent)
       a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-15/float(n))
       snew = float(a_new.sum())
       if snew <= 1e-15:
           break
       a = a_new / snew
       it +=1
       if noimp > patience:
           mult *= 0.7
           noimp = 0
           if mult < 0.2:
               a = best_a.copy()
               mult = 0.5
               noimp = 0
       if tag and (it - last_print >= 500):
           print(f"[{tag}] it={it} cur={sc:.10f} best={best_sc:.10f} str={strength:.3f} full={full}")
           last_print=it
    # final convert maybe
    return best_a, best_sc
```
`noimp` increments based on current sc vs best. If current not improving but maybe LSE improving, could reduce prematurely. But okay. We can also track if sc < best_sc + small tolerance? Use tolerance: if sc < best_sc - 1e-12 improve; else noimp. Good.

`_target_flatten`:
```
def _target_flatten(a0, seconds, tag='', mix=0.12, alpha0=0.04, patience=100):
   seconds=max...
   a=_normalize(a0, floor=1e-14/n)
   n; m1; t_start/end
   A=fft... c... best
   alpha=alpha0; mult=1; noimp=0; it=0
   while time<t_end:
      A...c... sc; update best
      frac...
      strength = max(0.005, alpha0*(1-0.5*frac)*mult)
      t = (1.0-mix)*c + mix/float(2*n-1)
      Bt=np.fft.rfft(t,m1); Bc=A*A
      ratio = Bt/(Bc + 1e-30)
      alt=np.zeros_like(ratio, dtype=np.complex128)
      mask = np.isfinite(ratio) & (np.abs(ratio) > 1e-12)
      alt[mask] = np.log(np.abs(ratio[mask])) + 1j*np.angle(ratio[mask])
      A = A * np.exp(0.5*strength*alt)
      a_new=np.fft.irfft(A,m1)[:n].real
      a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-15/n)
      snew=a_new.sum(); if snew<=0 break; a=a_new/snew
      it++
      if noimp>patience: mult*=0.7; noimp=0; if mult<0.1: break
      if tag and it%500==0: print...
   return best
```

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(a, n_new):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n_old=a.size
    if n_old == n_new: return _normalize(a, floor=1e-14/n_new)
    if n_old < 2: return np.ones(n_new)/n_new
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_old)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    y=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    if n_new > 5:
        kernel=np.array([0.125,0.25,0.25,0.25,0.125], dtype=np.float64)
        y=np.convolve(y, kernel, mode='same')
        y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
    return _normalize(y, floor=1e-14/float(n_new))
```
Interpolation endpoints okay. Smoothing kernel sum1; `np.convolve` with mode same may slightly change edges but okay.

`_one_sided`:
```
def _one_sided(n, alpha=0.5, delta=0.5, lam=0.0):
   idx=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
   x=(idx+delta)/max(1.0,float(n-1))
   v=np.power(x, -alpha)
   if lam !=0: v *= np.exp(lam*idx/(n-1))
   return _normalize(v, floor=1e-14/n)
```

```python
"""
Step-function height search for minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a)^2)

The implementation combines:
  1. A mirror/multiplicative LSE descent on the positive simplex. It builds a
     smooth soft-maximum of the convolution coefficients and applies entropy
     projection updates, which are well behaved for non-negative sequences.
  2. A Fourier-domain square-root projection toward a flatter coefficient
     vector for conv(a,a), used as a short shaping phase.
  3. Resampling to larger, FFT-friendly lengths and sparse active-peak
     variants for very large lengths.
  4. Conservative restarts and final polishing on the globally best candidate.

The function `propose_candidate` is the required entry point.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _pow2(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a, floor=0.0):
    """Clip to non-negative, optionally enforce a tiny floor, normalize sum=1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _fast_conv(a, m1=None):
    """Linear convolution a * a using FFT. Input should be non-negative."""
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if m1 is None:
        m1 = _pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m1)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m1)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _score(a):
    """Internal objective using FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    c = _fast_conv(a)
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(c.max()) / (s * s))


def _lse(c, beta):
    """Log-sum-exp smoothing of coefficients.

    Returns
        L   = max(c) + log(sum(exp(beta*(c-max(c)))))/beta
        p   = exp(beta*(c-max(c)))/sum(...)
        cmax= max(c)
    """
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        if c.size:
            p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return cmax, p, cmax

    e = np.exp(beta * (c - cmax))
    se = float(e.sum())
    if se <= 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        L = cmax
    else:
        p = e / se
        L = cmax + float(np.log(se)) / beta
    return L, p, cmax


def _grad_full(a, p, m2):
    """
    Gradient (up to a positive scale) of an LSE objective with weights p.

    For c = a*a, d c_k / d a_i = 2 a_{k-i} when 0 <= k-i < n.
    We compute g_i = 4*n*sum_k p_k a_{k-i}.
    """
    n = a.size
    PA = np.fft.rfft(p, m2)
    RA = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], m2)
    g = np.fft.irfft(PA * RA, m2)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return g


def _top_weights(c, K, beta):
    """Approximate LSE weights by using only the top K peaks."""
    L = c.size
    if K <= 0:
        K = 1
    if K >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L)
        w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        return idx, w

    idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    vals = c[idx]
    vmax = float(vals.max())
    w = np.exp(beta * (vals - vmax))
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sw):
        w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
    else:
        w = w / sw
    return idx, w


def _grad_sparse(a, idx, w):
    """Sparse gradient for a small top-peak set."""
    n = a.size
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    mult = 4.0 * float(n)
    for ii in range(idx.size):
        weight = float(w[ii])
        if weight == 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(idx[ii])
        js = k - n + 1
        if js < 0:
            js = 0
        je = k
        if je > n - 1:
            je = n - 1
        if je >= js:
            coef = mult * weight
            g[js:je + 1] += coef * a[js:je + 1][::-1]
    return g


def _target_flatten(a0, seconds, tag="", mix=0.10, alpha0=0.04, patience=120):
    """
    Short spectral shaping:
      In time domain, pull target convolution coefficients toward uniform.
      In frequency domain, take a square-root projection A <- A * (T/A^2)^alpha.
    """
    seconds = max(0.0, float(seconds))
    a = _normalize(a0, floor=1e-14 / max(1, a0.size))
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m1 = _pow2(L)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t_start + seconds

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m1)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m1)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(2.0 * n * c.max())

    if tag:
        print(f"[{tag}] flatten start {best_sc:.10f} n={n} limit={seconds:.0f}s")

    mult_factor = 1.0
    noimp = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = 0

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-9, seconds))

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, m1)
        c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m1)[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * c.max())

        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            noimp = 0
        else:
            noimp += 1

        strength = max(0.003, alpha0 * (1.0 - 0.5 * frac) * mult_factor)

        # Target coefficients, sum preserved.
        ttarget = (1.0 - mix) * c + mix / float(L)
        ttarget = np.maximum(ttarget, 0.0)
        s_t = float(ttarget.sum())
        if s_t <= 1e-15:
            break
        ttarget = ttarget / s_t

        Bt = np.fft.rfft(ttarget, m1)
        Bc = A * A

        ratio = Bt / (Bc + 1e-30)
        alt = np.zeros(ratio.shape, dtype=np.complex128)
        mask = np.isfinite(ratio) & (np.abs(ratio) > 1e-12)
        alt[mask] = np.log(np.abs(ratio[mask])) + 1j * np.angle(ratio[mask])

        A = A * np.exp(0.5 * strength * alt)
        a_new = np.fft.irfft(A, m1)[:n].real
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-15 / float(n))
        snew = float(a_new.sum())
        if snew <= 1e-15:
            break
        a = a_new / snew
        it += 1

        if noimp > patience:
            mult_factor *= 0.7
            noimp = 0
            if mult_factor < 0.10:
                break

        if tag and (it - last_print >= 1000):
            print(f"[{tag}] flatten it={it} cur={sc:.10f} best={best_sc:.10f}")
            last_print = it

    return best_a, best_sc


def _mlse(a0, seconds, tag="", strength0=0.18, patience=180, min_strength=0.01,
          use_full=True, beta_start=8.0, beta_end=40.0, sparse_k=None):
    """
    Multiplicative/LSE descent on the positive simplex.

    Uses the exact full LSE gradient when FFT size is modest; otherwise uses
    a sparse top-peak gradient for very large lengths.
    """
    seconds = max(0.0, float(seconds))
    if seconds <= 0.0:
        a = _normalize(a0)
        return a, _score(a)

    a = _normalize(a0, floor=1e-14 / max(1, a0.size))
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m1 = _pow2(L)
    m2 = _pow2(3 * n - 2)
    full = bool(use_full) and (m2 <= 32768) and (m1 <= 65536)

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t_start + seconds

    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m1)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m1)[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = float(2.0 * n * c.max())

    if tag:
        print(f"[{tag}] MLSE start {best_sc:.10f} n={n} full={full} limit={seconds:.0f}s")

    mult_factor = 1.0
    noimp = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = 0

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-9, seconds))

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, m1)
        c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m1)[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * c.max())

        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            noimp = 0
        else:
            noimp += 1

        beta = float(n) * (beta_start + (beta_end - beta_start) * frac)

        if full:
            _, p, _ = _lse(c, beta)
            g = _grad_full(a, p, m2)
        else:
            K = sparse_k
            if K is None:
                K = int(min(128, max(16, 0.002 * L)))
            idx, w = _top_weights(c, K, beta)
            g = _grad_sparse(a, idx, w)

        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        dot = float(np.dot(a, g))
        d = g - dot
        scale = float(np.std(d))

        if scale <= 1e-15:
            noimp += 5
            if noimp > patience:
                mult_factor *= 0.5
                noimp = 0
                if mult_factor < 0.10:
                    break
            continue

        base_strength = strength0 * (1.0 - 0.35 * frac)
        strength = max(min_strength, base_strength * mult_factor)

        exponent = -strength * d / scale
        exponent = np.clip(exponent, -0.6, 0.6)

        a_new = a * np.exp(exponent)
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-15 / float(n))
        snew = float(a_new.sum())
        if snew <= 1e-15:
            break
        a = a_new / snew
        it += 1

        if noimp > patience:
            mult_factor *= 0.7
            noimp = 0
            if mult_factor < 0.20:
                a = best_a.copy()
                mult_factor = 0.50
                noimp = 0

        if tag and (it - last_print >= 500):
            print(f"[{tag}] MLSE it={it} cur={sc:.10f} best={best_sc:.10f} "
                  f"strength={strength:.4f} full={full}")
            last_print = it

    return best_a, best_sc


def _resample(a, n_new):
    """Resample a height sequence to a new length, with mild smoothing."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n_old = a.size
    if n_old == 0:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n_new))
    if n_old == 1:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64)

    if n_old == n_new:
        return _normalize(a, floor=1e-14 / float(n_new))

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)

    if n_new > 5:
        kernel = np.array([0.125, 0.25, 0.25, 0.25, 0.125], dtype=np.float64)
        y = np.convolve(y, kernel, mode="same")
        y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)

    return _normalize(y, floor=1e-14 / float(n_new))


def _one_sided(n, alpha=0.5, delta=0.5, lam=0.0):
    """Fallback initializer with a mild one-sided singularity."""
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (idx + delta) / max(1.0, float(n - 1))
    v = np.power(np.maximum(x, 1e-12), -alpha)
    if lam != 0.0:
        v *= np.exp(lam * idx / max(1.0, float(n - 1)))
    return _normalize(v, floor=1e-14 / float(n))


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400):
    """Search and return the best non-negative height sequence found."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t0 + total_budget - 60.0

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size >= 128:
            parent = _normalize(p, floor=0.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        sc = _score(parent)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = parent.copy()
            print(f"[PARENT] score={sc:.10f} n={parent.size}")

    # Fallback initializer.
    if best_a is None:
        init_n = 10240
        a_init = _one_sided(init_n)
        sc = _score(a_init)
        best_sc = sc
        best_a = a_init
        print(f"[INIT-ONE-SIDED] score={sc:.10f} n={init_n}")

    n_target = 10240
    n_big = 16384

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 1: Short local improvement on the parent length.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if parent is not None and parent.size != n_target:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 900:
            secs = min(150.0, max(30.0, rem - 900.0))
            a_p, sc_p = _mlse(parent.copy(), secs, tag="M-P", strength0=0.12)
            if sc_p < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_p
                best_a = a_p.copy()
                print(f"[PARENT-POLISH] improved to {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 2: Main search at FFT-friendly larger length 10240.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 1100:
        source = parent if parent is not None else best_a
        a_t = _resample(source, n_target)

        sc_flat0 = _score(a_t)
        if sc_flat0 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_flat0
            best_a = a_t.copy()
            print(f"[TARGET-RESAMPLE] score={best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

        # Short spectral shaping.
        rem0 = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem0 > 1000:
            secs_f = min(70.0, max(20.0, rem0 - 1000.0))
            a_t, sc_t = _target_flatten(a_t, secs_f, tag="F-T", mix=0.10, alpha0=0.04)
            if sc_t < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_t
                best_a = a_t.copy()
                print(f"[TARGET-FLATTEN] improved to {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

        rem1 = deadline - time.monotonic()
        secs_main = min(1350.0, max(120.0, rem1 - 800.0))
        a_t, sc_t = _mlse(a_t, secs_main, tag="M-T", strength0=0.20, use_full=True)
        if sc_t < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_t
            best_a = a_t.copy()
            print(f"[TARGET-MLSE] improved to {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 3: Try a larger length with sparse active-peak MLSE.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 950 and best_a.size != n_big:
        source = best_a
        if parent is not None and source.size < n_target:
            source = parent
        a_b = _resample(source, n_big)

        sc_b0 = _score(a_b)
        if sc_b0 < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_b0
            best_a = a_b.copy()
            print(f"[BIG-RESAMPLE] score={best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

        secs_b = min(500.0, max(60.0, rem - 550.0))
        a_b, sc_b = _mlse(a_b, secs_b, tag="M-B", strength0=0.22,
                          use_full=False, sparse_k=80)
        if sc_b < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_b
            best_a = a_b.copy()
            print(f"[BIG-MLSE] improved to {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 4: Small controlled random perturbations of the best sequence.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 500 and best_a.size >= 512:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 17)
        for trial in range(2):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 260:
                break
            noise = 0.012 + 0.008 * trial
            a_p = best_a * (1.0 + noise * rng.standard_normal(best_a.size))
            a_p = _normalize(a_p, floor=1e-14 / float(a_p.size))

            secs_p = min(220.0, max(40.0, rem - 240.0))
            a_p, sc_p = _mlse(a_p, secs_p, tag=f"RESTART-{trial}", strength0=0.12)
            if sc_p < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc_p
                best_a = a_p.copy()
                print(f"[RESTART-{trial}] improved to {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 5: Final polish of the global best.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 180:
        secs_final = min(500.0, max(60.0, rem - 80.0))
        full_final = bool(best_a.size <= 10240)
        a_f, sc_f = _mlse(best_a.copy(), secs_final, tag="FINAL",
                          strength0=0.08, use_full=full_final,
                          sparse_k=64)
        if sc_f < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc_f
            best_a = a_f.copy()
            print(f"[FINAL] improved to {best_sc:.10f} n={best_a.size}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Prepare final list.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    out = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
        s = 1.0

    # Normalize to sum 1 for stable evaluation.
    out = out / s

    out_list = [float(x) for x in out]

    # Optional verification.
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
        print(f"[VERIFY] internal={best_sc:.10f} verified={verified:.10f} "
              f"n={len(out_list)} time={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[VERIFY] skipped/failed: {e}")
        print(f"[VERIFY] internal={best_sc:.10f} n={len(out_list)} "
              f"time={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s")

    return out_list
```
